## Backend Publish Flow Analysis - AgentCenter Nuxt

I've thoroughly explored the backend publish pipeline. Here's the complete breakdown:

---

### 1. INTERNAL PUBLISH ENDPOINTS (`server/api/internal/publish/`)

#### **create-draft.post.ts** (Lines 1-16)
- **Route**: `POST /api/internal/publish/create-draft`
- **Input**: `ManifestFormSchema` validated body
- **Handler**: Calls `createDraftExtension(userId, body)`
- **Key code**:
```typescript
const result = await createDraftExtension(user.id, body)
if (!result.ok) {
  throw createError({
    statusCode: result.error === "slug_taken" ? 409 : 400,
    statusMessage: result.error,
    data: result,
  })
}
```
- **Returns**: `{ ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }`

#### **get-draft.get.ts** (Lines 1-18)
- **Route**: `GET /api/internal/publish/get-draft?extensionId=<id>`
- **Input**: Query param `extensionId` (string, required)
- **Handler**: Calls `getDraftExtension(userId, extensionId)`
- **Returns**: `{ extensionId, versionId, bundleUploaded: boolean, formValues: ManifestFormValues }`

#### **update-draft.post.ts** (Lines 1-30)
- **Route**: `POST /api/internal/publish/update-draft`
- **Input**: `{ extensionId: string; values: ManifestFormSchema }`
- **Key validations**: Slug and version are **immutable** (lines 142-154 in publish.ts)
- **Returns**: `{ ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }`
- **Error handling**: Rejects if `visibility !== "draft"`, slug/version changed

#### **attach-file.post.ts** (Lines 1-27)
- **Route**: `POST /api/internal/publish/attach-file`
- **Input**: `{ versionId: uuid; r2Key: string; size: number; checksumSha256: string }`
- **Handler**: Calls `attachFile(userId, versionId, r2Key, size, checksumSha256)`
- **What happens**:
  - Creates a `files` record with `scanStatus: "pending"`
  - Links it to `extensionVersions.bundleFileId` (lines 293-306 in publish.ts)
- **Returns**: `{ ok: true; fileId: string }`

#### **submit.post.ts** (Lines 1-21)
- **Route**: `POST /api/internal/publish/submit`
- **Input**: `{ versionId: uuid }`
- **Handler**: Calls `submitForReview(userId, versionId)`
- **Key code** (lines 319-371 in publish.ts):
```typescript
export async function submitForReview(
  userId: string,
  versionId: string,
): Promise<SubmitResult> {
  // ... owner checks, bundle existence check ...
  await submit(versionId)  // transitions status pending → scanning
  const { inngest } = await import("~~/server/utils/inngest")
  await inngest.send({
    name: "extension/scan.requested",
    data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },
  })
}
```
- **Returns**: `{ ok: true }` on success
- **Errors**: `"not_found"` (404), `"no_bundle"` (400), `"version_not_submittable"` (409), `"scan_queue_unavailable"` (503)

#### **discard.post.ts** (Lines 1-17)
- **Route**: `POST /api/internal/publish/discard`
- **Input**: `{ extensionId: string }`
- **Handler**: Calls `discardDraft(userId, extensionId)`
- **Effect**: Deletes the entire extension record if `visibility === "draft"`

#### **my-extensions.get.ts** (Lines 1-6)
- **Route**: `GET /api/internal/publish/my-extensions`
- **Returns**: List of user's extensions with their latest version status

---

### 2. UPLOAD FLOW (`server/api/upload/` + `server/utils/storage.ts`)

#### **sign.post.ts** (Lines 1-23)
- **Route**: `POST /api/upload/sign`
- **Input**: `{ slug: string; version: string; contentType?: string }`
- **Handler**:
```typescript
const storage = await useStorage()
const key = storage.bundleKey(body.slug, body.version)  // → "bundles/{slug}/{version}/bundle.zip"
const url = await storage.getSignedUploadUrl(key, body.contentType)
return { url, key }
```
- **Returns**: `{ url: string (signed S3/R2 URL); key: string }`

#### **Storage Backend** (`server/utils/storage.ts`, Lines 1-94)
**Pluggable backends**: R2 (Cloudflare) preferred over Supabase

**Signed URL generation**:
- **R2 Backend** (lines 74-80):
```typescript
async getSignedUploadUrl(objectKey, contentType, expiresInSeconds = 300) {
  const command = new PutObjectCommand({
    Bucket: bucket,
    Key: objectKey,
    ContentType: contentType,
  })
  return getSignedUrl(client, command, { expiresIn: expiresInSeconds })
}
```
- **Supabase Backend** (lines 40-46):
```typescript
const { data, error } = await client.storage
  .from(bucket)
  .createSignedUploadUrl(objectKey, { upsert: true })
return data.signedUrl
```

#### **Upload Sequence**:
1. Client calls `POST /api/upload/sign` with `{ slug, version }`
2. Server returns `{ url (signed S3/R2 URL), key }`
3. Client uploads bundle directly to S3/R2 using signed URL
4. Client calls `POST /api/internal/publish/attach-file` with `{ versionId, r2Key, size, checksumSha256 }`
5. Server creates file record and links to version

---

### 3. MANIFEST PARSING (`server/utils/jobs/scan-bundle.ts`)

**Job**: `scanBundle` (Inngest function, lines 19-121)
- **Trigger**: `extension/scan.requested` event
- **Input**: `{ versionId: string; fileId: string }`

**Parsing pipeline** (lines 32-77):
```typescript
const scanResult = await step.run("download-and-scan", async () => {
  const fileRow = await filesRepo.findById(db, fileId)
  const storage = await useStorage()
  const signedUrl = await storage.getSignedDownloadUrl(fileRow.r2Key, 120)
  const res = await fetch(signedUrl)
  const uint8 = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer())
  
  // SHA-256 checksum validation
  const hashBuf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", uint8)
  const checksum = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuf))
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join("")

  // ZIP extraction
  let entries: ReturnType<typeof unzipSync>
  try {
    entries = unzipSync(uint8)
  } catch (err) {
    return { ok: false as const, reason: "invalid_zip", checksum }
  }
  
  // Extract & parse manifest.toml
  const manifestEntry = entries["manifest.toml"]
  if (!manifestEntry) {
    return { ok: false as const, reason: "missing_manifest", checksum }
  }
  let raw: unknown
  try {
    raw = parse(new TextDecoder().decode(manifestEntry))
  } catch {
    return { ok: false as const, reason: "invalid_toml", checksum }
  }
  
  // Validate against BundleManifestSchema
  const parsed = BundleManifestSchema.safeParse(raw)
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      ok: false as const,
      reason: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "schema_error",
      checksum,
    }
  }
  return { ok: true as const, checksum }
})
```

**Scan report** (lines 79-84):
```typescript
const scanReport = {
  manifestOk: scanResult.ok,
  reason: scanResult.ok ? null : scanResult.reason,
  checksum: scanResult.checksum,
  scannedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
}
```

**Result recording** (lines 86-100):
- Calls `recordScanResult(versionId, fileId, result)` which updates `files.scanStatus` and `files.scanReport`
- Applies scope-based state machine decision (see **Scope-based Publishing** below)

---

### 4. BACKGROUND JOBS PIPELINE

**Event sequence** (all Inngest jobs):

#### **Job 1: `extension/scan.requested`** → `scanBundle`
- **Triggered by**: `submitForReview()` in `submit.post.ts`
- **What it does**:
  1. Downloads bundle from storage (via signed URL)
  2. Validates ZIP integrity
  3. Extracts and parses `manifest.toml`
  4. Validates manifest against `BundleManifestSchema`
  5. Records scan result (creates `scanReport` JSON)
  6. Applies scope-based outcome (personal scope auto-publishes; org/enterprise waits)
  7. **Sends next event**: `extension/index.requested`

#### **Job 2: `extension/index.requested`** → `reindexSearch`
- **Triggered by**: `scanBundle.sendEvent()` at line 114-117
- **What it does**:
  1. Calls `publishVersion(versionId)` → stamps `publishedAt` on extension + version (org/enterprise admin approval flow)
  2. **Sends next event**: `extension/published`
- **Code** (lines 22-23 in reindex-search.ts):
```typescript
const result = await step.run("publish-version", () => publishVersion(versionId))
```

#### **Job 3: `extension/published`** (event only, no handler yet)
- **Triggered by**: `reindexSearch.sendEvent()` at line 35-38
- **Data**: `{ extensionId, versionId }`
- **Purpose**: Notification hook for future analytics/webhook purposes

**Job pipeline order**:
```
submitForReview()
  ↓
[EVENT] extension/scan.requested (versionId, fileId)
  ↓
scanBundle job (downloads, validates manifest, scans)
  ↓
recordScanResult() (updates file scan status, applies scope decision)
  ↓
[EVENT] extension/index.requested (versionId)
  ↓
reindexSearch job (publishVersion for org/enterprise)
  ↓
[EVENT] extension/published (extensionId, versionId)
```

### 5. VALIDATORS & SCHEMAS (`shared/validators/manifest.ts`)

#### **ManifestFormSchema** (lines 55-75)
Form fields collected by the publish wizard:
```typescript
export const ManifestFormSchema = z.object({
  slug: z.string().min(3).max(64).regex(SLUG_PATTERN),
  name: z.string().min(2).max(80),
  nameZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),
  version: z.string().regex(SEMVER_PATTERN),  // "Must be semver (e.g. 1.0.0)"
  category: z.enum(["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"]),
  scope: z.enum(["personal", "org", "enterprise"]),
  summary: z.string().min(1).max(80),  // One-liner, lands in `tagline` column
  taglineZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),
  readmeMd: z.string().max(16_000).optional(),
  iconColor: z.enum(["indigo", "amber", "emerald", "rose", "slate"]).default("indigo"),
  tagIds: z.array(z.string()).max(8),
  deptId: z.string().optional(),
  permissions: PermissionsSchema,
  sourceMethod: z.literal("zip").default("zip"),
})
```

#### **PermissionsSchema** (lines 35-42)
```typescript
export const PermissionsSchema = z
  .object({
    network: z.boolean().optional(),
    files: z.boolean().optional(),
    runtime: z.boolean().optional(),
    data: z.boolean().optional(),
  })
  .default({})
```

#### **BundleManifestSchema** (lines 84-101)
Bundle-side manifest (from actual ZIP, not wizard):
```typescript
export const BundleManifestSchema = z.object({
  extension: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({
    slug: true,
    name: true,
    nameZh: true,
    version: true,
    category: true,
    scope: true,
    description: true,  // REQUIRED (not in wizard)
    descriptionZh: true,
    tagline: true,
  }),
  categorization: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({
    funcCat: true,
    subCat: true,
    l2: true,
  }),
})
```

#### **Slug & Version Patterns** (lines 4-5)
```typescript
export const SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/
export const SEMVER_PATTERN = /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/
```

**Key columns for publishing**:

```typescript
export const extensions = pgTable("extensions", {
  id: text().primaryKey(),
  slug: text().notNull().unique(),
  name: text().notNull(),
  nameZh: text(),
  tagline: text(),  // Maps to "summary" from wizard
  taglineZh: text(),
  category: extensionCategoryEnum(),  // "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
  scope: extensionScopeEnum(),  // "personal" | "org" | "enterprise"
  funcCat: funcCatEnum(),  // "workTask" | "business" | "tools" (nullable, server-defaults)
  subCat: text(),
  l2: text(),
  deptId: text(),
  iconColor: text(),
  readmeMd: text(),
  permissions: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
  publisherUserId: text(),  // FK → users.id
  ownerOrgId: text().notNull(),  // FK → organizations.id
  visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),  // "draft" | "published" | "archived"
  featured: boolean().notNull().default(false),
  publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),  // Set by state machine
  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
})
```

#### **extensionVersions table** (lines 145-168)
```typescript
export const extensionVersions = pgTable("extension_versions", {
  id: text().primaryKey(),
  extensionId: text().notNull().references(() => extensions.id),
  version: text().notNull(),  // Semver
  status: versionStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),  // "pending" | "scanning" | "ready" | "rejected"
  bundleFileId: text(),  // FK-ish to files.id (no FK constraint to avoid circular refs)
  sourceMethod: text().notNull().default("zip"),
  sourceMeta: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
  manifestJson: jsonb(),
  changelog: text(),
  changelogZh: text(),
  publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),  // Set when status → ready (personal) or by admin
  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
})
```
**Unique constraint**: `(extensionId, version)` — only one version per semver per extension

#### **files table** (lines 170-182)

```typescript
  r2Key: text().notNull(),  // "bundles/{slug}/{version}/bundle.zip"
  size: bigint({ mode: "bigint" }).notNull(),
  checksumSha256: text().notNull(),  // From upload + validated during scan
  mimeType: text(),
  scanStatus: fileScanStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),  // "pending" | "clean" | "flagged"

})
```

```typescript
})
```
Primary key: `(extensionId, tagId)` — many-to-many

### 7. STATE MACHINE ORCHESTRATION (`shared/extensions/state.ts` + `server/utils/extensions-state.ts`)

#### **Scope-based Publishing Decision** (`shared/extensions/state.ts`, lines 39-70)
```typescript
export function decideScanOutcome(input: {
  scope: ExtensionScope  // "personal" | "org" | "enterprise"
  result: ScanResult  // { ok, checksum, scanReport } or { ok: false, reason, scanReport }
  now: Date
}): ScanDecision {
  const { scope, result, now } = input

  // Failed scan: always reject
  if (!result.ok) {
    return {
      file: { scanStatus: "flagged", scanReport: result.scanReport },
      version: { status: "rejected" },
    }
  }

  // Success: personal auto-publishes, org/enterprise waits for admin
  const isPersonal = scope === "personal"
  const publishedAt = isPersonal ? now : null

  const decision: ScanDecision = {
    file: {
      scanStatus: "clean",
      scanReport: result.scanReport,
      checksumSha256: result.checksum,
    },
    version: { status: "ready", publishedAt },
  }

  if (isPersonal) {
    decision.extension = { visibility: "published", publishedAt: now }
  }

  return decision
}
```

**Key insight**: 
- **Personal scope**: Scan passes → immediately `published`
- **Org/Enterprise scope**: Scan passes → `ready`, waits for admin `publishVersion()` call

#### **Status Transitions** (`server/repositories/versions.ts`, lines 170-186)
```typescript
export async function updateStatusGuarded(
  db: Transactable,
  versionId: string,
  spec: { from: VersionStatus[]; to: VersionStatus },
): Promise<{ updated: boolean }> {
  const rows = await db
    .update(extensionVersions)
    .set({ status: spec.to })
    .where(
      and(
        eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),
        inArray(extensionVersions.status, spec.from),
      ),
    )
    .returning({ id: extensionVersions.id })
  return { updated: rows.length > 0 }
}
```

---

### SUMMARY DIAGRAM

```
User publishes extension
          ↓
POST /api/internal/publish/create-draft (ManifestFormSchema)
  → Creates extensions + extensionVersions (status: pending, visibility: draft)
          ↓
POST /api/upload/sign (slug, version)
  → Returns signed S3/R2 URL for bundle.zip upload
          ↓
Client uploads bundle.zip directly to S3/R2
          ↓
POST /api/internal/publish/attach-file (versionId, r2Key, size, checksumSha256)
  → Creates files record (scanStatus: pending), links to extensionVersions.bundleFileId
          ↓
POST /api/internal/publish/submit (versionId)
  → Transitions extensionVersions.status: pending → scanning
  → Sends EVENT: extension/scan.requested
          ↓
[JOB] scanBundle (Inngest)
  → Downloads from storage, unzips, validates manifest.toml against BundleManifestSchema
  → Calculates SHA-256, records scanReport
  → Applies decideScanOutcome(scope, result):
      - Personal: extension → published immediately, version → ready with publishedAt
      - Org/Enterprise: version → ready (no publishedAt), extension stays draft
  → Sends EVENT: extension/index.requested
          ↓
[JOB] reindexSearch (Inngest)
  → For org/enterprise: admin approval flow (publishVersion stamps publishedAt on both)
  → Sends EVENT: extension/published
          ↓
User can download published extension
```

---

## Comprehensive Publish Flow Analysis

I've completed a thorough exploration of the AgentCenter publish flow. Here's the full breakdown:

---

### 1. **PUBLISH PAGES & ROUTE STRUCTURE**

**Files:**
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/publish/index.vue` (lines 1-77)
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/publish/new.vue` (lines 1-16)
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/publish/[id]/edit.vue` (lines 1-65)

**Route structure:**
- `/publish` — Dashboard showing user's extensions and drafts (index.vue)
- `/publish/new` — Create new draft wizard (new.vue → UploadWizard)
- `/publish/[id]/edit` — Resume/edit existing draft (edit.vue → UploadWizard)

All routes require auth middleware: `require-auth` and `require-onboard`.

The publish pages are minimal wrappers; all wizard logic lives in the **UploadWizard** component.

---

### 2. **PUBLISH COMPONENTS**

**Key files under `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/publish/`:**

| Component | Responsibility | Key Props/State |
|-----------|-----------------|-----------------|
| **UploadWizard.vue** (lines 1-126) | Main orchestrator. Manages step state, validates per-step, routes to correct step component, handles navigation. | Props: `init?: WizardInit` (extensionId, versionId, bundleUploaded, formValues). Uses `usePublishWizard` composable. Returns a 5-element STEPS array (0=basics, 1=bundle, 2=listing, 3=review, 4=success). |
| **WizardBasics.vue** (lines 1-120) | Step 0. Collects name, slug, version, summary; optional Chinese translations; category (skills/mcp/slash/plugins); scope (personal/org/enterprise). | Props: `wizard: PublishWizard`. Reactive: `form`, `isLocked`. Auto-derives slug from name. |
| **WizardBundle.vue** (lines 1-144) | Step 1. File upload with drag-drop. Validates zip type, max 50MB. Computes SHA256, signs upload, PUT to R2, attaches to version. | Props: `wizard: PublishWizard`. Local: `uploading`, `fileName`, `localError`. Calls `/api/upload/sign` then `/api/internal/publish/attach-file`. |
| **WizardListing.vue** (lines 1-173) | Step 2. Icon color picker (indigo/amber/emerald/rose/slate), tags (max 8), README markdown, permissions toggles (network/files/runtime/data), and dept field. | Props: `wizard: PublishWizard`. Local: `tagInput`, computed `remainingSuggestions`. Tag normalization (trim, lowercase, replace spaces). |
| **WizardReview.vue** (lines 1-90) | Step 3. Summary view of all filled fields (name, slug, version, summary, category, scope, bundle status, tags, dept). Each row has an "Edit" button that emits jump event to go back to that step. | Props: `wizard: PublishWizard`. Emits: `jump(step)`. Computed `rows` array mapping each field to its source step. Also renders scope-based notes (personal vs org/enterprise). |
| **WizardSuccess.vue** (lines 1-31) | Step 4 (final). Confirmation screen with checkmark. Different messages for personal vs shared scopes. Links back to dashboard. | Props: `wizard: PublishWizard`. No complexity — just presentation. |
| **WizardLivePreview.vue** (lines 1-97) | Right sidebar. Renders a card preview (icon, name, category, summary, tags) and a derived manifest JSON based on current form state. | Props: `form: ManifestFormValues`. Computed: `manifestJson` (stringified with proper structure for bundle). Shows first 4 tags + overflow count. |
| **PubField.vue** (lines 1-26) | Wrapper for form fields. Renders label, required indicator, hint text, and slot for input. | Props: `label`, `hint?`, `required?`, `optional?`. Styling only. |
| **PubChoice.vue** (lines 1-54) | Radio-button style choice component (used for category, scope, permissions). Active state with checkmark. Optional badge and description. | Props: `value`, `current?`, `title`, `desc?`, `badge?`, `disabled?`. Emits: `select(value)`. |
| **DiscardButton.vue** (lines 1-40) | Discard draft button with confirmation. Calls `/api/internal/publish/discard`. | Props: `extensionId`, `extensionName`. Emits: `discarded`. Local: `busy`. |
| **DashboardSkeleton.vue** | Placeholder skeleton while fetching extensions. | Props: `rows`. |

---

### 3. **STEP FLOW (Complete State Machine)**

**From UploadWizard.vue lines 11-16:**

```typescript
const STEPS: Array<{ index: WizardStep; key: string }> = [
  { index: 0, key: "basics" },
  { index: 1, key: "bundle" },
  { index: 2, key: "listing" },
  { index: 3, key: "review" },
]
```

**Step Sequence:**

1. **Step 0: BASICS** (WizardBasics)
   - Collects: name, slug (auto-derived), version (default 1.0.0), summary, category, scope, optional Chinese name/summary
   - Validation: name 2-80 chars, slug 3-64 chars lowercase+digits+hyphens, semver format, summary 1-80 chars
   - On advance: Calls `/api/internal/publish/create-draft` (first time) or `/api/internal/publish/update-draft` (resume)
   - Creates extension & version records; locks slug & version for edit flows

2. **Step 1: BUNDLE** (WizardBundle)
   - Collects: .zip file (max 50MB)
   - Validation: file exists, is zip, under size limit
   - On advance: Simple validation that `bundleUploaded === true`
   - Bundle upload process:
     - Compute SHA256 hash client-side
     - GET signed URL from `/api/upload/sign` with slug+version
     - PUT file to R2 (Cloudflare)
     - POST to `/api/internal/publish/attach-file` with r2Key, size, checksum → links file to version

3. **Step 2: LISTING** (WizardListing)
   - Collects: icon color (enum 5 colors), tags (array, max 8), README markdown (max 16KB), permissions object (4 toggles), optional dept ID
   - Validation: tagIds.length > 0, readmeMd.length > 0
   - On advance: No API call, just step transition

4. **Step 3: REVIEW** (WizardReview)
   - Display-only summary of all fields
   - Each field has "Edit" link that calls `jumpTo(step)` to go back
   - Shows scope-dependent note (personal = auto-publishes, org/enterprise = awaits review)
   - On advance (submit): 
     - Final validation that `canSubmit === true` (all steps valid + bundle uploaded)
     - POST to `/api/internal/publish/update-draft` (in case listing was edited after review jump)
     - POST to `/api/internal/publish/submit` with versionId
     - Backend triggers `extension/scan.requested` Inngest event
     - Step moves to 4 (success)

5. **Step 4: SUCCESS** (WizardSuccess)
   - Final screen with checkmark
   - No further transitions

**Validity gates (from UploadWizard.vue lines 18-25):**

```typescript
const nextDisabled = computed(() => {
  if (wizard.busy.value) return true
  if (wizard.step.value === 0) return !wizard.basicsValid.value
  if (wizard.step.value === 1) return !wizard.bundleValid.value
  if (wizard.step.value === 2) return !wizard.listingValid.value
  if (wizard.step.value === 3) return !wizard.canSubmit.value
  return true
})
```

---

### 4. **STATE MANAGEMENT (Composable-based)**

**Main composable:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/composables/usePublishWizard.ts`

**Form state (reactive):**
```typescript
const form = reactive<ManifestFormValues>({
  slug: "",
  name: "",
  nameZh: undefined,
  version: "1.0.0",
  category: "skills",
  scope: "personal",
  summary: "",
  taglineZh: undefined,
  readmeMd: undefined,
  iconColor: "indigo",
  tagIds: [],
  deptId: undefined,
  permissions: {},
  sourceMethod: "zip",
})
```

**Wizard lifecycle state (refs):**
```typescript
const extensionId = ref<string | null>(init.extensionId ?? null)
const versionId = ref<string | null>(init.versionId ?? null)
const bundleUploaded = ref(init.bundleUploaded ?? false)
const step = ref<WizardStep>(init.bundleUploaded ? 3 : init.extensionId ? 1 : 0)
const error = ref<WizardError | null>(null)
const busy = ref(false)
const isLocked = computed(() => extensionId.value !== null)
```

**Step initialization logic (lines 51):**
- If resuming an edit and bundle already uploaded → start at step 3 (review)
- If resuming and no bundle → start at step 1 (bundle)
- Otherwise → start at step 0 (basics)

**Auto-slug derivation (lines 58-68):**
```typescript
let lastAutoSlug = slugFromName(form.name)
watch(
  () => form.name,
  (name) => {
    const next = slugFromName(name)
    if (form.slug === lastAutoSlug || form.slug === "") {
      form.slug = next
      lastAutoSlug = next
    }
  },
)
```
Only re-derives if user hasn't hand-edited slug.

**Validity computeds (lines 73-91):**
- `basicsValid`: all basic fields meet constraints
- `bundleValid`: `bundleUploaded === true`
- `listingValid`: tagIds.length > 0 AND readmeMd.length > 0
- `canSubmit`: all three above valid

**Step transitions (lines 103-171):**

```typescript
async function advanceFromBasics(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!basicsValid.value) return false
  clearError()
  busy.value = true
  try {
    if (!extensionId.value) {
      const result = await $fetch<{ ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }>(
        "/api/internal/publish/create-draft",
        { method: "POST", body: form },
      )
      extensionId.value = result.extensionId
      versionId.value = result.versionId
    } else {
      await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/update-draft", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { extensionId: extensionId.value, values: form },
      })
    }
    step.value = 1
    return true
  } catch (err) {
    const data = (err as { data?: { error?: string; detail?: string } })?.data
    setError(data?.error ?? "unknown_error", data?.detail)
    return false
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}

function advanceFromBundle() {
  if (!bundleValid.value) return false
  step.value = 2
  return true
}

function advanceFromListing() {
  if (!listingValid.value) return false
  step.value = 3
  return true
}

async function submit(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!canSubmit.value || !extensionId.value || !versionId.value) return false
  clearError()
  busy.value = true
  try {
    await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/update-draft", {
      method: "POST",
      body: { extensionId: extensionId.value, values: form },
    })
    await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/submit", {
      method: "POST",
      body: { versionId: versionId.value },
    })
    step.value = 4
    return true
  } catch (err) {
    const data = (err as { data?: { error?: string; detail?: string }; statusMessage?: string })
      ?.data
    setError(
      data?.error ?? (err as { statusMessage?: string })?.statusMessage ?? "unknown_error",
      data?.detail,
    )
    return false
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}
```

**No Pinia store** — all state is in the composable. UploadWizard instantiates once per page load. State is lost on navigation (by design; drafts are persisted server-side).

---

### 5. **VALIDATION (Zod Schemas)**

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/validators/manifest.ts`

**Core schema used by both bundle and form:**
```typescript
export const SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/
export const SEMVER_PATTERN = /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/
export const SUMMARY_MAX = 80

export const ExtensionManifestCore = z.object({
  slug: z.string().min(3).max(64).regex(SLUG_PATTERN, "Lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens only"),
  name: z.string().min(2).max(80),
  nameZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),
  version: z.string().regex(SEMVER_PATTERN, "Must be semver (e.g. 1.0.0)"),
  category: z.enum(["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"]),
  scope: z.enum(["personal", "org", "enterprise"]),
  description: z.string().min(1).max(280),
  descriptionZh: z.string().max(280).optional(),
  // ... more fields ...
})
```

**Wizard form schema (lines 44-75):**
```typescript
export const ManifestFormSchema = z.object({
  slug: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.slug,
  name: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.name,
  nameZh: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.nameZh,
  version: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.version,
  category: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.category,
  scope: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.scope,
  summary: z.string().min(1, "Required").max(SUMMARY_MAX),
  taglineZh: z.string().max(SUMMARY_MAX).optional(),
  readmeMd: z.string().max(16_000).optional(),
  iconColor: z.enum(["indigo", "amber", "emerald", "rose", "slate"]).default("indigo"),
  tagIds: z.array(z.string()).max(8),
  deptId: z.string().optional(),
  permissions: PermissionsSchema,
  sourceMethod: z.literal("zip").default("zip"),
})

export type ManifestFormValues = z.infer<typeof ManifestFormSchema>
```

**Permissions schema:**
```typescript
export const PermissionsSchema = z
  .object({
    network: z.boolean().optional(),
    files: z.boolean().optional(),
    runtime: z.boolean().optional(),
    data: z.boolean().optional(),
  })
  .default({})
```

Notably: `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` (function categorization) are **not** in the wizard form. They're server-side defaults computed from the chosen `category` (see `/server/utils/publish.ts` lines 20-31):
- mcp → funcCat: "tools", subCat: "integrations"
- slash → funcCat: "tools", subCat: "commands"
- default → funcCat: "tools", subCat: "general"

**Bundle manifest schema (lines 84-101):**
```typescript
export const BundleManifestSchema = z.object({
  extension: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({
    slug: true, name: true, nameZh: true, version: true, category: true, scope: true,
    description: true, descriptionZh: true, tagline: true,
  }),
  categorization: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({
    funcCat: true, subCat: true, l2: true,
  }),
})
```
Bundle still carries `description` (long form), `funcCat`, `subCat` — these come from the `.zip` manifest, not from the wizard.

---

### 6. **SERVER-SIDE API LAYER**

**Endpoints under `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/publish/`:**

| Endpoint | Method | Body | Response | Logic |
|----------|--------|------|----------|-------|
| `/api/internal/publish/create-draft.post.ts` | POST | ManifestFormSchema (form fields) | `{ ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }` | Inserts extensions row (draft visibility), extensionVersions row (pending status), extensionTags rows. Returns 409 on slug conflict. |
| `/api/internal/publish/update-draft.post.ts` | POST | `{ extensionId: string; values: ManifestFormSchema }` | Same as create | Updates extension (name, summary, category, scope, tags, etc). Slug & version immutable. Validates ownership & draft visibility. |
| `/api/internal/publish/get-draft.get.ts` | GET | Query: `extensionId` | `{ extensionId, versionId, bundleUploaded, formValues: ManifestFormValues }` | Rehydrates draft for edit page. Returns 404 if not owned or not draft. |
| `/api/internal/publish/attach-file.post.ts` | POST | `{ versionId, r2Key, size, checksumSha256 }` | `{ ok: true; fileId: string }` | Creates files row, updates extensionVersions.bundleFileId. Links the uploaded ZIP to the version. |
| `/api/internal/publish/submit.post.ts` | POST | `{ versionId: string (uuid) }` | `{ ok: true }` | Calls `submit(versionId)` state machine. Enqueues `extension/scan.requested` Inngest event. Returns 503 if queue unavailable. |
| `/api/internal/publish/my-extensions.get.ts` | GET | — | `{ items: [] }` | Returns extensions for current user with latest version status. Used by dashboard. |
| `/api/internal/publish/discard.post.ts` | POST | `{ extensionId: string }` | `{ ok: true }` | Soft delete: removes extension only if draft visibility. |

All endpoints validate user ownership and proper state before mutating.

---

### 7. **KEY PERSISTENCE FLOW**

**New draft creation** (UploadWizard → Basics advance):
1. Frontend validates basics (name, slug, version, summary, category, scope)
2. POST form to `/api/internal/publish/create-draft`
3. Backend: Insert extensions row (visibility="draft", publisherUserId=user), extensionVersions row (status="pending", sourceMethod="zip"), extensionTags rows
4. Return extensionId, versionId to frontend
5. Frontend stores IDs in composable, moves to step 1

**Draft update** (any step or resume):
1. POST `{ extensionId, values: form }` to `/api/internal/publish/update-draft`
2. Backend: Update extensions row (mutable fields only), diff-replace tags
3. Return versionId

**Bundle upload** (Bundle step):
1. Client: Compute SHA256
2. POST form data to `/api/upload/sign` → get signed R2 URL
3. PUT file to R2
4. POST `{ versionId, r2Key, size, checksumSha256 }` to `/api/internal/publish/attach-file`
5. Backend: Create files row, link to extensionVersions.bundleFileId

**Submit for review** (Review step → Submit button):
1. Final `/api/internal/publish/update-draft` (in case listing was edited)
2. POST `{ versionId }` to `/api/internal/publish/submit`
3. Backend: Call `submit(versionId)` state machine, enqueue scan event
4. Frontend: Move to step 4 (success)

---

  }

---

This is the complete, stateful publish flow. The wizard is composable-driven (not Pinia), uses Zod for both client and server validation, and orchestrates a multi-step form with progressive persistence (create on step 0 advance, update on any other step). All state is lost on page leave but drafts persist server-side.

```
1	import type { ManifestFormValues } from "~~/shared/validators/manifest"
2	import { SLUG_PATTERN, SEMVER_PATTERN, SUMMARY_MAX } from "~~/shared/validators/manifest"
3	
4	export type WizardStep = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4
5	
6	export interface WizardError {
7	  msg: string
8	  detail?: string
9	}
10	
11	export interface WizardInit {
12	  extensionId?: string
13	  versionId?: string
14	  bundleUploaded?: boolean
15	  formValues?: Partial<ManifestFormValues>
16	}
17	
18	const DEFAULT_FORM: ManifestFormValues = {
19	  slug: "",
20	  name: "",
21	  nameZh: undefined,
22	  version: "1.0.0",
23	  category: "skills",
24	  scope: "personal",
25	  summary: "",
26	  taglineZh: undefined,
27	  readmeMd: undefined,
28	  iconColor: "indigo",
29	  tagIds: [],
30	  deptId: undefined,
31	  permissions: {},
32	  sourceMethod: "zip",
33	}
34	
35	export function slugFromName(name: string): string {
36	  return name
37	    .toLowerCase()
38	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
39	    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
40	    .slice(0, 40)
41	}
42	
43	export function usePublishWizard(init: WizardInit = {}) {
44	  const form = reactive<ManifestFormValues>({
45	    ...DEFAULT_FORM,
46	    ...init.formValues,
47	  })
48	  const extensionId = ref<string | null>(init.extensionId ?? null)
49	  const versionId = ref<string | null>(init.versionId ?? null)
50	  const bundleUploaded = ref(init.bundleUploaded ?? false)
51	  const step = ref<WizardStep>(init.bundleUploaded ? 3 : init.extensionId ? 1 : 0)
52	  const error = ref<WizardError | null>(null)
53	  const busy = ref(false)
54	
55	  // Auto-derive slug from name as long as the user hasn't hand-edited slug.
56	  // We track that by snapshotting the auto-derived value and only re-deriving
57	  // when slug still matches the last auto-derived snapshot.
58	  let lastAutoSlug = slugFromName(form.name)
59	  watch(
60	    () => form.name,
61	    (name) => {
62	      const next = slugFromName(name)
63	      if (form.slug === lastAutoSlug || form.slug === "") {
64	        form.slug = next
65	        lastAutoSlug = next
66	      }
67	    },
68	  )
69	
70	  const isLocked = computed(() => extensionId.value !== null)
71	
72	  // Per-step validity. Same gates as the original wizard.
73	  const basicsValid = computed(() => {
74	    if (form.name.length < 2 || form.name.length > 80) return false
75	    if (!SLUG_PATTERN.test(form.slug) || form.slug.length < 3 || form.slug.length > 64) return false
76	    if (!SEMVER_PATTERN.test(form.version)) return false
77	    if (form.summary.length < 1 || form.summary.length > SUMMARY_MAX) return false
78	    return true
79	  })
80	
81	  const bundleValid = computed(() => bundleUploaded.value)
82	
83	  const listingValid = computed(() => {
84	    if (form.tagIds.length === 0) return false
85	    if ((form.readmeMd ?? "").length < 1) return false
86	    return true
87	  })
88	
89	  const canSubmit = computed(
90	    () => basicsValid.value && bundleValid.value && listingValid.value,
91	  )
92	
93	  function setError(msg: string, detail?: string) {
94	    error.value = { msg, detail }
95	  }
96	
97	  function clearError() {
98	    error.value = null
99	  }
100	
101	  // --- Step transitions ---
102	
103	  async function advanceFromBasics(): Promise<boolean> {
104	    if (!basicsValid.value) return false
105	    clearError()
106	    busy.value = true
107	    try {
108	      if (!extensionId.value) {
109	        const result = await $fetch<{ ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }>(
110	          "/api/internal/publish/create-draft",
111	          { method: "POST", body: form },
112	        )
113	        extensionId.value = result.extensionId
114	        versionId.value = result.versionId
115	      } else {
116	        await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/update-draft", {
117	          method: "POST",
118	          body: { extensionId: extensionId.value, values: form },
119	        })
120	      }
121	      step.value = 1
122	      return true
123	    } catch (err) {
124	      const data = (err as { data?: { error?: string; detail?: string } })?.data
125	      setError(data?.error ?? "unknown_error", data?.detail)
126	      return false
127	    } finally {
128	      busy.value = false
129	    }
130	  }
131	
132	  function advanceFromBundle() {
133	    if (!bundleValid.value) return false
134	    step.value = 2
135	    return true
136	  }
137	
138	  function advanceFromListing() {
139	    if (!listingValid.value) return false
140	    step.value = 3
141	    return true
142	  }
143	
144	  async function submit(): Promise<boolean> {
145	    if (!canSubmit.value || !extensionId.value || !versionId.value) return false
146	    clearError()
147	    busy.value = true
148	    try {
149	      // Final persistence in case Listing was edited after Review jump-back.
150	      await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/update-draft", {
151	        method: "POST",
152	        body: { extensionId: extensionId.value, values: form },
153	      })
154	      await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/submit", {
155	        method: "POST",
156	        body: { versionId: versionId.value },
157	      })
158	      step.value = 4
159	      return true
160	    } catch (err) {
161	      const data = (err as { data?: { error?: string; detail?: string }; statusMessage?: string })
162	        ?.data
163	      setError(
164	        data?.error ?? (err as { statusMessage?: string })?.statusMessage ?? "unknown_error",
165	        data?.detail,
166	      )
167	      return false
168	    } finally {
169	      busy.value = false
170	    }
171	  }
172	
173	  function jumpTo(target: WizardStep) {
174	    step.value = target
175	  }
176	
177	  function markBundleUploaded() {
178	    bundleUploaded.value = true
179	  }
180	
181	  return {
182	    form,
183	    step,
184	    extensionId,
185	    versionId,
186	    bundleUploaded,
187	    error,
188	    busy,
189	    isLocked,
190	    basicsValid,
191	    bundleValid,
192	    listingValid,
193	    canSubmit,
194	    advanceFromBasics,
195	    advanceFromBundle,
196	    advanceFromListing,
197	    submit,
198	    jumpTo,
199	    markBundleUploaded,
200	    setError,
201	    clearError,
202	  }
203	}
204	
205	export type PublishWizard = ReturnType<typeof usePublishWizard>
206	
```

```
1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Check, Upload } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import type { PublishWizard } from "~/composables/usePublishWizard"
4	
5	const props = defineProps<{ wizard: PublishWizard }>()
6	
7	const { t } = useI18n()
8	
9	const MAX_SIZE = 50 * 1024 * 1024 // 50 MB
10	const fileInput = useTemplateRef<HTMLInputElement>("fileInput")
11	const fileName = ref<string | null>(null)
12	const localError = ref<string | null>(null)
13	const uploading = ref(false)
14	
15	async function sha256Hex(buf: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
16	  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf)
17	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash))
18	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
19	    .join("")
20	}
21	
22	async function onFile(file: File) {
23	  localError.value = null
24	  if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".zip")) {
25	    localError.value = t("publish.wizard.bundle.wrongType")
26	    return
27	  }
28	  if (file.size > MAX_SIZE) {
29	    localError.value = t("publish.wizard.bundle.tooLarge")
30	    return
31	  }
32	  if (!props.wizard.versionId.value || !props.wizard.extensionId.value) {
33	    props.wizard.setError("no_draft")
34	    return
35	  }
36	
37	  uploading.value = true
38	  try {
39	    const buf = await file.arrayBuffer()
40	    const checksum = await sha256Hex(buf)
41	
42	    const signed = await $fetch<{ url: string; key: string }>(
43	      "/api/upload/sign",
44	      {
45	        method: "POST",
46	        body: {
47	          slug: props.wizard.form.slug,
48	          version: props.wizard.form.version,
49	          contentType: "application/zip",
50	        },
51	      },
52	    )
53	
54	    const put = await fetch(signed.url, {
55	      method: "PUT",
56	      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/zip" },
57	      body: buf,
58	    })
59	    if (!put.ok) throw new Error(`upload failed: ${put.status}`)
60	
61	    await $fetch("/api/internal/publish/attach-file", {
62	      method: "POST",
63	      body: {
64	        versionId: props.wizard.versionId.value,
65	        r2Key: signed.key,
66	        size: file.size,
67	        checksumSha256: checksum,
68	      },
69	    })
70	
71	    fileName.value = file.name
72	    props.wizard.markBundleUploaded()
73	  } catch (err) {
74	    const data = (err as { data?: { error?: string }; statusMessage?: string })
75	    localError.value = data?.data?.error ?? data?.statusMessage ?? t("publish.errors.generic")
76	  } finally {
77	    uploading.value = false
78	  }
79	}
80	
81	function onFileChange(event: Event) {
82	  const target = event.target as HTMLInputElement
83	  const file = target.files?.[0]
84	  if (file) onFile(file)
85	  // Reset so picking the same file again still fires `change` — common when an
86	  // upload fails and the user retries with the same .zip.
87	  target.value = ""
88	}
89	
90	function onDrop(event: DragEvent) {
91	  event.preventDefault()
92	  const file = event.dataTransfer?.files?.[0]
93	  if (file) onFile(file)
94	}
95	
96	function pick() {
97	  fileInput.value?.click()
98	}
99	</script>
100	
101	<template>
102	  <div class="space-y-6">
103	    <header>
104	      <h2 class="font-serif text-xl text-(--color-ink)">{{ t("publish.wizard.bundle.title") }}</h2>
105	      <p class="mt-1 text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)">
106	        {{ t("publish.wizard.bundle.subtitle") }}
107	      </p>
108	    </header>
109	
110	    <div
111	      class="rounded-(--radius-card) border-2 border-dashed border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-8 text-center transition-colors hover:border-(--color-accent)/40"
112	      @dragover.prevent
113	      @drop="onDrop"
114	    >
115	      <div v-if="wizard.bundleUploaded.value && fileName" class="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
116	        <Check class="size-8 text-(--color-accent)" aria-hidden="true" />
117	        <p class="text-sm text-(--color-ink)">
118	          {{ t("publish.wizard.bundle.uploaded", { name: fileName }) }}
119	        </p>
120	        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" @click="pick">
121	          {{ t("publish.wizard.bundle.replace") }}
122	        </Button>
123	      </div>
124	      <div v-else class="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
125	        <Upload class="size-8 text-(--color-ink-muted)" aria-hidden="true" />
126	        <p class="text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)">{{ t("publish.wizard.bundle.drop") }}</p>
127	        <p class="text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted)">{{ t("publish.wizard.bundle.max") }}</p>
128	        <Button :disabled="uploading" @click="pick">
129	          {{ uploading ? t("publish.stages.uploading") : t("publish.fields.bundle") }}
130	        </Button>
131	      </div>
132	
133	      <input
134	        ref="fileInput"
135	        type="file"
136	        accept=".zip,application/zip"
137	        class="hidden"
138	        @change="onFileChange"
139	      >
140	    </div>
141	
142	    <p v-if="localError" class="text-sm text-red-600">{{ localError }}</p>
143	  </div>
144	</template>
145
```

```
1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import type { PublishWizard } from "~/composables/usePublishWizard"
3	import { ICON_COLOR_KEYS, ICON_COLORS, type IconColor } from "~~/shared/data/icon-colors"

7	const { t } = useI18n()
8	const form = props.wizard.form
9	
10	const SUGGESTED_TAGS = ["stable", "beta", "official", "ai", "search", "data", "productivity"]
11	const TAG_LIMIT = 8
12	
13	const tagInput = ref("")
14	
15	function addTag(raw: string) {
16	  const trimmed = raw.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-")
17	  if (!trimmed) return
18	  if (form.tagIds.length >= TAG_LIMIT) return
19	  if (form.tagIds.includes(trimmed)) return
20	  form.tagIds.push(trimmed)
21	  tagInput.value = ""
22	}
23	
24	function removeTag(tag: string) {
25	  form.tagIds = form.tagIds.filter((t) => t !== tag)
26	}
27	
28	function onTagKey(event: KeyboardEvent) {
29	  if (event.key === "Enter") {
30	    event.preventDefault()
31	    addTag(tagInput.value)
32	    return
33	  }
34	  if (event.key === "Backspace" && tagInput.value === "" && form.tagIds.length > 0) {
35	    form.tagIds.pop()
36	  }
37	}
38	
39	const remainingSuggestions = computed(() =>
40	  SUGGESTED_TAGS.filter((s) => !form.tagIds.includes(s)),
41	)
42	
43	const PERMISSION_KEYS = ["network", "files", "runtime", "data"] as const
44	type PermKey = (typeof PERMISSION_KEYS)[number]
45	
46	function togglePerm(key: PermKey) {
47	  form.permissions = { ...form.permissions, [key]: !form.permissions[key] }
48	}
49	
50	function setIconColor(color: IconColor) {
51	  form.iconColor = color
52	}
```

Perfect! I have all the information I need. Let me compile a comprehensive report.

## Comprehensive Analysis of AgentCenter Next.js Publish Wizard

### 1. PUBLISH FLOW LOCATION & STRUCTURE

**Route**: `/[locale]/publish/new`

**File locations:**
- **Main wizard component**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/index.tsx`
- **Page entry point**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/app/[locale]/publish/new/page.tsx`
- **Shared form components**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/shared.tsx`
- **Steps directory**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/steps/`

**Step components:**
1. `basics.tsx` - Step 1: Name, slug, version, summary, category (type), scope, optional Chinese translation
2. `source.tsx` - Step 2: Bundle upload (ZIP, Git coming soon, CLI)
3. `listing.tsx` - Step 3: Icon color, tags, department, README, permissions
4. `review.tsx` - Step 4: Read-only summary of all data with inline edit buttons

---

### 2. STEP RAIL / PROGRESS INDICATOR (MOST CRITICAL)

**Location**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/index.tsx`, lines 304-348

**Full JSX & styling markup:**

```jsx
<div className="hidden flex-col gap-1 lg:flex">
  {STEP_KEYS.map((key, i) => {
    const done = i < step;
    const active = i === step;
    return (
      <button
        key={key}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => i <= step && setStep(i as StepIndex)}
        disabled={i > step}
        className={cn(
          "flex items-start gap-3 rounded-md px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors",
          active && "bg-primary/[0.07]",
          i > step && "cursor-not-allowed",
        )}
      >
        <span
          className={cn(
            "mt-0.5 inline-flex h-[22px] w-[22px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold",
            active || done
              ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
              : "border-[1.5px] border-border text-muted-foreground",
          )}
        >
          {done ? <Check aria-hidden className="size-3" /> : i + 1}
        </span>
        <span className="flex flex-col">
          <span
            className={cn(
              "text-[13px]",
              active
                ? "font-bold text-foreground"
                : "font-medium text-muted-foreground",
            )}
          >
            {tw(`${key}Title`)}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground">
            {tw(`${key}Desc`)}
          </span>
        </span>
      </button>
    );
  })}
</div>
```

**Visual Design Details:**
- **Container**: `200px` fixed width sidebar (part of 3-column grid), hidden on mobile (only visible `lg:flex`)
- **Each step button**: Flex layout with fixed-width step indicator + label stack
  - **Gap**: `gap-3` between step circle and text
  - **Padding**: `px-3 py-2.5` (compact)
  - **Border radius**: `rounded-md`
  - **Hover/active background**: `bg-primary/[0.07]` (7% opacity of primary color)
  - **Transition**: `transition-colors`
  - **Disabled state**: `cursor-not-allowed` for future steps

- **Step indicator circle** (the number or checkmark):
  - **Size**: `22px × 22px` (h-[22px] w-[22px])
  - **Shape**: `rounded-full` (perfect circle)
  - **Position offset**: `mt-0.5` (slight margin-top for vertical alignment)
  - **Font**: `text-[11px] font-bold`
  - **Active/completed states**:
    - Background filled with primary color: `bg-primary text-primary-foreground`
    - Shows checkmark icon (`<Check>` from lucide) when completed
    - Shows step number (1, 2, 3, 4) when active/upcoming
  - **Inactive/upcoming states**:
    - Border only: `border-[1.5px] border-border`
    - Transparent background
    - Text color: `text-muted-foreground`

- **Step label stack** (title + description):
  - **Title**: `text-[13px]`, font-bold when active else font-medium, color toggles between `text-foreground` (active) and `text-muted-foreground` (inactive)
  - **Description**: `text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground` (always muted)
  - **Gap**: `flex flex-col` with no explicit gap (uses defaults)

**Key interactions:**
- Buttons for completed/current steps are clickable (can navigate backward/to completed steps)
- Future steps are `disabled` (cannot click ahead)
- No connecting lines between steps; each is visually independent
- NO animations/transitions on step changes (instant)

---

### 3. THE STEPS THEMSELVES

#### **STEP 1: BASICS** (`basics.tsx`, lines 64-202)

**File path**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/steps/basics.tsx`

**What it collects:**
- `name` (string, required) → auto-slugifies into `slug`
- `slug` (string, required, locked after first save)
- `version` (string, required, locked after first save)
- `summary` (string, required)
- `category` (type: skills/mcp/slash/plugins, required)
- `scope` (personal/org/enterprise, required)
- `nameZh`, `taglineZh` (optional, bilingual support)

**Layout**: Single column, flex stack with 5px gaps

**Field structure** (all use the shared `PubField` wrapper):

```jsx
<PubField label={t("name")} hint={t("nameHint")} required>
  <PubInput
    value={draft.name}
    onChange={setName}
    placeholder={t("namePlaceholder")}
  />
</PubField>
```

**PubField markup** (shared.tsx, lines 10-44):
```jsx
<div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
  <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
    <label className="text-[13px] font-semibold">
      {label}
      {required && <span className="ml-0.5 text-destructive">*</span>}
    </label>
    {optional && (
      <span className="text-[11px] font-normal text-muted-foreground">
        {optional}
      </span>
    )}
  </div>
  {children}
  {hint && (
    <div className="text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
      {hint}
    </div>
  )}
</div>
```

**PubInput markup** (shared.tsx, lines 48-78):
```jsx
<input
  type={type}
  value={value}
  onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
  placeholder={placeholder}
  readOnly={readOnly}
  className={cn(
    "w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 text-[13px] outline-none transition-shadow",
    "focus:border-primary focus:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_color-mix(in_oklch,var(--primary)_18%,transparent)]",
    readOnly && "cursor-not-allowed bg-muted text-muted-foreground",
    mono && "font-mono",
  )}
/>
```

**Input field styling:**
- **Width**: Full width (`w-full`)
- **Padding**: `px-3 py-2` (12px horizontal, 8px vertical)
- **Border**: `border border-border` (thin 1px)
- **Border radius**: `rounded-md`
- **Font**: `text-[13px]`
- **Focus state**: `focus:border-primary` + color-mix shadow (3px spread, 18% primary opacity)
- **Read-only state**: `bg-muted text-muted-foreground cursor-not-allowed`
- **Monospace variant**: `font-mono` class applied to slug/version fields

**Category (Type) selector** (lines 102-138):
```jsx
<PubField label={tType("label")} required>
  <div
    role="radiogroup"
    aria-label={tType("label")}
    className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-4"
  >
    {types.map(({ k, icon: Icon }) => {
      const active = draft.category === k;
      return (
        <button
          key={k}
          type="button"
          role="radio"
          aria-checked={active}
          onClick={() => patch({ category: k })}
          className={cn(
            "flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[1.5px] px-2 py-3.5 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-all",
            active
              ? "border-primary bg-primary/[0.05] text-primary"
              : "border-border bg-card text-foreground hover:border-primary/40",
          )}
        >
          <Icon
            aria-hidden
            className={cn(
              "size-[18px]",
              active ? "text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground",
            )}
          />
          <span>{tType(k)}</span>
        </button>
      );
    })}
  </div>
</PubField>
```

**Type cards styling:**
- **Grid**: 2 columns on mobile, 4 on tablet+ (`grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-4`)
- **Each card**: Vertical flex, centered text
  - Border: `border-[1.5px]` (thicker than inputs)
  - Border radius: `rounded-lg`
  - Padding: `px-2 py-3.5`
  - Font: `text-[12px] font-semibold`
  - Icon: `size-[18px]` (18×18px)
  - Gap: `gap-1.5` between icon and label
  - **Active state**: `border-primary bg-primary/[0.05] text-primary` (light fill + primary text)
  - **Inactive state**: `border-border bg-card text-foreground hover:border-primary/40` (dark border, light on hover)

**Scope selector** (lines 140-168):
Uses `PubChoice` component for each option (personal/org/enterprise). Stacked vertically with `flex flex-col gap-2.5`.

**PubChoice markup** (shared.tsx, lines 110-167):
```jsx
<button
  type="button"
  role="radio"
  aria-checked={active}
  onClick={onClick}
  disabled={disabled}
  className={cn(
    "flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border border-[1.5px] px-3.5 py-3 text-left transition-all",
    active
      ? "border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]"
      : "border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/40",
    disabled && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-60",
  )}
>
  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
    <span className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-foreground">
      {title}
    </span>
    <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
      {badge && (
        <span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}
      {active && (
        <span className="inline-flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground">
          <Check aria-hidden className="size-2.5" />
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  </div>
  {desc && (
    <span className="text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
      {desc}
    </span>
  )}
</button>
```

**Choice card styling:**
- **Padding**: `px-3.5 py-3`
- **Border**: `border-[1.5px]`
- **Border radius**: `rounded-lg`
- **Layout**: Flex column with title/badge row + optional description
- **Title**: `text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-foreground`
- **Description**: `text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground`
- **Check icon**: `h-4 w-4` circle, `bg-primary`, contains `size-2.5` checkmark
- **Badge** (optional): `rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px]` (muted pill)
- **Active state**: `border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]`
- **Inactive state**: `border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/40`

#### **STEP 2: SOURCE** (`source.tsx`, lines 26-327)

**File path**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/steps/source.tsx`

**What it collects:**
- `sourceMethod` (zip | git | cli)
- Bundle file (ZIP upload via drag-drop or browse)
- File must be `.zip`, max 50MB

**Layout**: Single column, tab-based content

**Method selector** (lines 178-207):
```jsx
<PubField label={t("methodLabel")} required>
  <div
    role="radiogroup"
    aria-label={t("methodLabel")}
    className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-3"
  >
    <PubChoice
      value="zip"
      current={tab}
      onClick={() => setTab("zip")}
      title={t("zipTitle")}
      desc={t("zipDesc")}
    />
    <PubChoice
      value="git"
      current={tab}
      onClick={() => setTab("git")}
      title={t("gitTitle")}
      desc={t("gitDesc")}
      badge={t("comingSoon")}
    />
    <PubChoice
      value="cli"
      current={tab}
      onClick={() => setTab("cli")}
      title={t("cliTitle")}
      desc={t("cliDesc")}
    />
  </div>
</PubField>
```

Three option cards in responsive grid (1 column mobile, 3 on tablet+).

**ZIP Upload dropzone** (lines 209-279):

```jsx
<div
  onClick={() => versionId && fileRef.current?.click()}
  onDragOver={(e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
  }}
  onDrop={(e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const f = e.dataTransfer.files?.[0];
    if (f) void handleFile(f);
  }}
  className={cn(
    "flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed p-8 transition-all",
    progress === "done"
      ? "border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]"
      : "border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/40",
    !versionId && "cursor-not-allowed opacity-60",
    versionId && progress !== "done" && "cursor-pointer",
  )}
>
  {progress === "done" ? (
    <>
      <Check aria-hidden className="size-8 text-primary" />
      <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold">
        {fileName ?? tu("uploaded")}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          setProgress("idle");
          setFileName(null);
          if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = "";
        }}
        className="text-[12px] text-muted-foreground underline transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
      >
        {t("replace")}
      </button>
    </>
  ) : progress === "uploading" ? (
    <>
      <Upload
        aria-hidden
        className="size-7 animate-pulse text-primary"
      />
      <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{tu("uploading")}</div>
    </>
  ) : (
    <>
      <Upload aria-hidden className="size-7 text-muted-foreground" />
      <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{t("dropHere")}</div>
      <div className="rounded-md bg-primary px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground">
        {t("orBrowse")}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted-foreground">
        {t("zipHint")}
      </div>
    </>
  )}
  <input
    ref={fileRef}
    type="file"
    accept=".zip"
    className="hidden"
    onChange={(e) => {
      const f = e.target.files?.[0];
      if (f) void handleFile(f);
    }}
  />
</div>
```

**Dropzone styling:**
- **Border**: `border-2 border-dashed` (thicker, dashed)
- **Padding**: `p-8` (generous 32px)
- **Border radius**: `rounded-xl` (large radius)
- **Layout**: Centered flex column, `gap-2.5`
- **Hover background**: `hover:border-primary/40` (lighter primary on hover)
- **States:**
  - **Idle (drop/upload state)**: `border-border bg-card`
    - Icon: `Upload` icon, `size-7 text-muted-foreground`
    - Main text: "Drop here", `text-[14px] font-semibold`
    - CTA button: `rounded-md bg-primary px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground` ("Or browse")
    - Hint text: `text-[11.5px] text-muted-foreground`
  - **Uploading**: 
    - Icon: `Upload` icon with `animate-pulse` (pulsing), `size-7 text-primary`
    - Text: "Uploading...", `text-[14px] font-semibold`
  - **Done**:
    - Icon: `Check` checkmark, `size-8 text-primary` (larger)
    - File name: `text-[14px] font-semibold` (file name or fallback "Uploaded")
    - Replace button: `text-[12px] text-muted-foreground underline` with hover underline
    - Background: `border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]` (light primary tint)

```jsx
          type="button"

        >
          {copied === i ? (
            <>
              <Check aria-hidden className="size-3 text-primary" />
              {t("copied")}
            </>
          ) : (
            t("copy")
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  ))}
</div>
```

- **Copy button**: `rounded border border-border px-2 py-0.5 font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground`

**Tags input** (lines 104-163):
```jsx
<PubField
  label={t("tags")}
  hint={t("tagsCount", { count: tags.length, max: 8 })}
  required
>
  <div className="flex min-h-[42px] flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-border bg-card p-2">
    {tags.map((tag) => (
      <span
        key={tag}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-primary/10 py-1 pl-2.5 pr-1 font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-primary"
      >
        {tag}
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={`Remove ${tag}`}
          onClick={() => removeTag(tag)}
          className="inline-flex p-0.5 text-primary hover:opacity-70"
        >
          <X aria-hidden className="size-3" />
        </button>
      </span>
    ))}
    <input
      value={tagInput}
      onChange={(e) => setTagInput(e.target.value)}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === "Enter") {
          e.preventDefault();
          addTag(tagInput);
          setTagInput("");
        } else if (
          e.key === "Backspace" &&
          tagInput === "" &&
          tags.length > 0
        ) {
          removeTag(tags[tags.length - 1]);
        }
      }}
      disabled={tagsAtLimit}
      placeholder={
        tagsAtLimit ? t("tagsLimitReached") : t("tagsPlaceholder")
      }
      className="min-w-[120px] flex-1 border-none bg-transparent px-1.5 py-1 text-[13px] outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
    />
  </div>
  {suggested.length > 0 && !tagsAtLimit && (
    <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
      {suggested.slice(0, 6).map((s) => (
        <button
          key={s}
          type="button"
          onClick={() => addTag(s)}
          className="rounded border border-dashed border-border px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-foreground"
        >
          + {s}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  )}
</PubField>
```

**Tags container:**
- **Border**: `border border-border`
- **Background**: `bg-card`
- **Padding**: `p-2` (8px)
- **Border radius**: `rounded-md`
- **Min height**: `min-h-[42px]` (allows single line)
- **Layout**: Flex wrap with `gap-1.5`

**Each tag pill:**
- **Background**: `bg-primary/10` (10% primary)
- **Text**: `font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-primary`
- **Padding**: `py-1 pl-2.5 pr-1` (asymmetric for icon button)
- **Border radius**: `rounded-md`
- **Remove button**: `inline-flex p-0.5 text-primary hover:opacity-70`, contains `X` icon `size-3`

**Text input (inside tags container):**
- **Min width**: `min-w-[120px]` (flexible growth)
- **Flex**: `flex-1` (grows to fill)
- **Border**: `border-none` (seamless)
- **Background**: `bg-transparent`
- **Padding**: `px-1.5 py-1`
- **Font**: `text-[13px]`
- **Disabled placeholder** (at max tags): Shows "Tags limit reached"
- **Backspace behavior**: Deletes last tag when input is empty

**Suggested tags** (below):
- **Layout**: `flex flex-wrap gap-1`
- **Each suggestion button**: `rounded border border-dashed border-border px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground`
  - Hover: `hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-foreground`
- **Shows**: First 6 suggested tags, only if space available

**Department dropdown** (lines 165-299):
Uses `DeptDropdown` sub-component with Popover (shadcn/ui):

```jsx
<Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
  <PopoverTrigger
    render={
      <button
        type="button"
        className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2.5 rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 text-[13px] outline-none transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 focus:border-primary"
      >
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 truncate">
          <Building2
            aria-hidden
            className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground"
          />
          <span className={cn(!value && "text-muted-foreground")}>
            {value ? labelPath : placeholder}
          </span>
        </span>
        <ChevronDown
          aria-hidden
          className="size-3 text-muted-foreground"
        />
      </button>
    }
  />
  <PopoverContent
    align="start"
    sideOffset={4}
    className="max-h-[260px] w-[var(--anchor-width)] overflow-auto p-1"
  >
    {flat.map((d) => (
      <button
        key={d.id}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          onChange(d.id);
          setOpen(false);
        }}
        style={{ paddingLeft: 10 + d.depth * 16 }}
        className={cn(
          "flex w-full items-center rounded px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-[12.5px] transition-colors",
          value === d.id
            ? "bg-primary/10 font-semibold text-primary"
            : "text-foreground hover:bg-accent",
        )}
      >
        {d.name}
      </button>
    ))}
  </PopoverContent>
</Popover>
```

**Trigger button styling:**
- **Border**: `border border-border`
- **Background**: `bg-card`
- **Padding**: `px-3 py-2`
- **Border radius**: `rounded-md`
- **Font**: `text-[13px]`
- **Hover**: `hover:border-primary/40`
- **Focus**: `focus:border-primary`
- **Icon**: `Building2`, `size-3.5 text-muted-foreground`
- **Chevron**: `ChevronDown`, `size-3 text-muted-foreground`

**Popover content:**
- **Max height**: `max-h-[260px]` with `overflow-auto`
- **Width**: `w-[var(--anchor-width)]` (matches trigger button)
- **Padding**: `p-1` (minimal)
- **Each option**: `px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] rounded`
  - **Selected**: `bg-primary/10 font-semibold text-primary`
  - **Unselected**: `text-foreground hover:bg-accent`
  - **Nesting**: Indentation via `paddingLeft: 10 + d.depth * 16` (10px base + 16px per depth level)

### 4. NAVIGATION & INTERACTION

**File path**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/index.tsx`, lines 390-427

**Navigation footer** (appears on all step pages):

```jsx
<div className="mt-7 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border pt-5">
  <div className="text-[12px] text-muted-foreground">
    {tw("stepIndicator", { current: step + 1, total: STEP_KEYS.length })}
  </div>
  <div className="flex gap-2">
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1) as StepIndex)}
      disabled={step === 0 || busy}
      className="rounded-md border border-border bg-transparent px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-muted disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
    >
      {tw("backButton")}
    </button>
    {step < 3 ? (
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          if (!stepsValid[step] || busy) return;
          if (step === 0) void advanceFromBasics();
          else setStep((s) => (s + 1) as StepIndex);
        }}
        disabled={!stepsValid[step] || busy}
        className="rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
      >
        {tw("nextButton")}
      </button>
    ) : (
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => void submitFinal()}
        disabled={busy || !versionId || !bundleUploaded}
        className="rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
      >
        {busy ? tw("submitting") : tw("submitButton")}
      </button>
    )}
  </div>
</div>
```

**Navigation bar styling:**
- **Top margin**: `mt-7` (spacing from content)
- **Top border**: `border-t border-border`
- **Top padding**: `pt-5` (spacing after border)
- **Layout**: Flex justify-between (`flex items-center justify-between`)

**Step indicator text** (left side):
- **Font**: `text-[12px] text-muted-foreground`
- **Content**: "Step X of Y" (localized, e.g., "Step 1 of 4")

**Button group** (right side):
- **Layout**: `flex gap-2` (horizontal row with 8px gap)

**Back button:**
- **Styling**: Secondary variant
  - Border: `border border-border`
  - Background: `bg-transparent`
  - Padding: `px-4 py-2`
  - Border radius: `rounded-md`
  - Font: `text-[13px] font-semibold`
  - Hover: `hover:bg-muted` (subtle background on hover)
  - Disabled: `disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40` (40% opacity)
  - Transition: `transition-colors`
- **Disabled**: Always on Step 1, or when busy (loading)

**Next/Submit button:**
- **Styling**: Primary variant
  - Background: `bg-primary`
  - Padding: `px-5 py-2` (slightly wider than back button)
  - Border radius: `rounded-md`
  - Font: `text-[13px] font-semibold`
  - Text color: `text-primary-foreground`
  - Hover: `hover:opacity-90` (slight transparency fade)
  - Disabled: `disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40`
  - Transition: `transition-opacity`

  - Step 4 (Review): "Submit" button (or "Submitting..." when busy)
- **Disabled logic:**

  - Step 2 (Source): Disabled until bundle is uploaded
  - Step 3 (Listing): Disabled until tags are added and README is filled

## SUMMARY: VISUAL & INTERACTION HIGHLIGHTS

**Most polished design elements:**

1. **Step rail indicator** — Clean circular badges (22px) with checkmarks, no connecting lines, light active background
2. **Choice cards** — Subtle primary borders with light background tint on active state, smooth transitions
3. **Tags input** — Seamless integration of tags and input field within a single bordered container, Enter/Backspace keyboard support
4. **Zip upload dropzone** — Large dashed border (2px), centered content, state-specific icons (Upload with pulse, Check), "Or browse" CTA button
5. **Permission checkboxes** — Custom checkbox styling with primary color on active, smooth transitions, semantic `aria-pressed`
6. **Review step** — 3-column grid layout for row-based summaries with "Edit" buttons that navigate back to steps
7. **Live preview sidebar** — Sticky positioning, dual sections (card preview + JSON manifest), real-time updates
8. **Overall spacing** — Consistent use of 2.5/5/6 multipliers (gaps, padding)
9. **Color system** — OKLCH-based icon palette with precise values, primary/muted-foreground toggles for all states
10. **Focus states** — Color-mix shadow for inputs (`0_0_0_3px_color-mix(in_oklch,var(--primary)_18%,transparent)`)

**Key interactions:**
- No animations between steps
- Instant state changes (no fade-ins)
- Pulsing only on upload (one animation used sparingly)
- Hover states with opacity or border color changes
- Disabled states at 40% opacity
- Keyboard support: Tab, Enter (tags), Backspace (tags), Escape (dropdowns implied)

---

```
9	
10	interface DraftResponse {
11	  extensionId: string
12	  versionId: string
13	  bundleUploaded: boolean
14	  formValues: ManifestFormValues
15	}
16	
17	const id = computed(() => String(route.params.id ?? ""))
18	
19	const { data, error } = await useFetch<DraftResponse>(
20	  "/api/internal/publish/get-draft",
21	  { query: computed(() => ({ extensionId: id.value })) },
22	)
23	
24	// Preserve the backend's status code so a 400 / 409 / auth failure doesn't
25	// get rewritten as 404. The error-boundary renders the matching message.
26	if (error.value) {
27	  const statusCode = error.value.statusCode ?? 500
28	  throw createError({
29	    statusCode,
30	    statusMessage: error.value.statusMessage ?? t("publish.errors.generic"),
31	  })
32	}
33	if (!data.value) {
34	  throw createError({
35	    statusCode: 404,
36	    statusMessage: t("publish.errors.notFound"),
37	  })
38	}
39	</script>
40	
41	<template>
42	  <div class="px-6 py-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
```

```
58	// Hardcoded for now; comes from the authenticated user's defaultDeptId once auth lands (Phase 6).
59	export const MY_DEPT_ID = "eng.cloud.infra";
60	
61	export function findDept(
62	  id: string,
63	  list: Department[] = DEPARTMENTS,
64	): Department | null {
65	  for (const d of list) {
66	    if (d.id === id) return d;
67	    if (d.children) {
68	      const found = findDept(id, d.children);
69	      if (found) return found;
70	    }
71	  }
72	  return null;
73	}
74	
75	export function deptPath(id: string, locale: Locale): string[] {
76	  const parts = id.split(".");
77	  const path: string[] = [];
78	  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
79	    const d = findDept(parts.slice(0, i + 1).join("."));
80	    if (d) path.push(locale === "zh" ? d.nameZh : d.name);
81	  }
82	  return path;
83	}
84	
85	export function isDescendant(deptId: string, ancestorId: string): boolean {
86	  return deptId === ancestorId || deptId.startsWith(ancestorId + ".");
87	}
```

> AGENT

Plan is written. Here's the summary before you approve.

**Goal:** bring the Nuxt publish wizard's *style and interaction* up to match the original at `agentcenter.vercel.app` (whose source I found locally at `../agentcenter`). Same 4-step flow — visual/interaction polish only, no flow or backend changes.

**The main gaps I'm closing** (Nuxt now → original):
- Bare horizontal numbered rail → **vertical clickable step rail** (circle + title + description, checkmark on done, click to revisit completed steps)
- Single column → **3-column carded layout** `[200px_1fr_320px]` with sticky preview
- Plain `<h1>` → header with back link, subtitle, and a **Save Draft** button
- Plain category choices → **icon cards**; richer dropzone (pulse/“Or browse”); 64px icon avatar + ring swatches
- **A Department dropdown that's currently missing entirely** (the form/review/preview already reference `deptId`, but there's no input)
- Flat review list → **grouped Summary cards + green/amber publish-status banner**; **“Step X of Y”** footer; polished success screen

**Feasibility confirmed:** `Popover`/`Checkbox`/`Button`/`Tabs` primitives exist, `shared/data/departments.ts` provides the dept tree + helpers, `PubField`/`PubChoice` are already faithful ports to reuse, and ZH has parallel publish keys.

**Two judgment calls baked in:**
- I'll respect CLAUDE.md decision #11 by keeping solid accent fill for the primary button only and using light tints for selection states (consistent with the existing wizard).
- Source-method tabs (git/CLI) and any flow-reorder/manifest auto-extraction are explicitly **out of scope**.
